Let's define helpers:
```
def _next_pow2(m):...
def _self_conv(a):...
def _cross(a,w): # returns B_i for score gradient
   if size 0 return empty
   M = a.size + w.size -1
   N=next_pow2(M)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],N)
   W=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
   full=np.fft.irfft(A*W,N)[:M]
   return full[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Need ensure `_cross` matches derivative. Earlier derived B_i=conv(a_rev,w)[n-1:2n-1]. We'll use.
- `_score(a)`:
```
n=len; S=sum; if S<... return inf
c=_self_conv(a)
# max maybe c.max
return 2*n*c.max()/S/S
```
- `_lse_score_grad(a, beta, pen=0.0)`:

Detailed `_local_pipeline`:
```
def _local_pipeline(a0, deadline, label, rng, base_beta_levels=None):
    a = _norm(a0)
    best_a = a.copy(); best_sc = _score(a)
    print start
    # Quick pre-polish: subgrad 5 iters
    # L-BFGS beta levels: maybe [20,50,100,300,1000,3000,10000]
    # Allocate time among levels: decreasing? More time at mid/high.
    # For each level, run up to 2-3 L-BFGS calls. If no improvement after one call, maybe still next.
    remaining = max(5, deadline - time.monotonic())
    weights = [0.04,0.06,0.08,0.12,0.2,0.28,0.22] normalize to remaining*0.8 (reserve 20% for subgrad)
    for beta,w in levels:
       budget = w*remaining
       if budget <10 continue
       n_calls = max(1, min(3, int(budget/20)))
       for each call: _run_lbfgs(a,beta,pen=1e-8,time=budget/n_calls,maxfun= maybe 800 depending n)
       if improved print
    # exact subgrad polish with remaining time (maybe 15 iters)
    return best
```

Detailed implementations:
```
def _norm(a, target=1.0):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return a
    a=np.nan_to_num(a,nan=0.0,posinf=1e6,neginf=0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,None)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <=1e-12: return np.full(a.size,target/a.size)
    return a*(target/s)
```

`_next_pow2`:
```
m=int(max(1,m)); return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```
Note for m=1 -> (0).bit_length()=0 ->1.

`_self_conv`:
```
a=np.asarray...; n=a.size; if n==0 return empty
M=2*n-1; N=_next_pow2(M)
A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
out=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:M]
return np.nan_to_num(out,nan=0.0,posinf=0.0,neginf=0.0)
```

`_cross_corr`:
```
a=...; w=...; n=a.size; if n==0 or w.size==0 return empty(n)
M=n+w.size-1; N=_next_pow2(M)
Ar=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],N); W=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
out=np.fft.irfft(Ar*W,N)[:M]
res=out[n-1:2*n-1]
return np.nan_to_num(res,...)
```
Need if n=0.

`_score_full`:
```
def _score_full(a):
    a=_norm? Should we norm? For score raw. Use raw, but ensure no nan:
    a=np.asarray... clip nan; S=sum; if S<... return inf,...
    c=_self_conv(a)
    d=float(len(a))*c/(S*S)
    dm=float(d.max()) if c.size else 0
    sc=2.0*dm if dm finite else inf
    return float(sc), c, d
```
Since d = n c/S^2, score = 2 max d. Good.

`_score(a)` just `_score_full(a)[0]`.

`_obj_lse`:
```
def _obj_lse(x,beta,pen):
   n=x.size
   if n==0: return 1e10, np.array([])
   x=np.nan_to_num(x,...)
   S=float(x.sum())
   if S<1e-8: return 1e10, np.zeros(n)
   c=_self_conv(np.asarray(x,float))
   d=float(n)*c/(S*S)
   dmax=float(d.max()) if c.size else 0.0
   if not finite: return 1e10, zeros
   # stable LSE for d
   z=beta*(d-dmax)
   z=np.clip(z,-745.0,0.0)
   e=np.exp(z)
   se=float(e.sum())
   if se<=0 or not finite:
       w=np.zeros_like(d); im=int(np.argmax(d)); w[im]=1.0; logse=0.0
   else:
       w=e/se; logse=float(np.log(se))
   val=2.0*(dmax + logse/beta)
   B=_cross_corr(x,w)
   LDS=float(w @ d)
   grad=4.0*float(n)*B/(S*S) - 4.0*LDS/S
   if pen>0:
      val += pen*(S-1.0)**2
      gp=2.0*pen*(S-1.0)
      grad = grad + gp
   grad=nan_to_num...
   return float(val), grad
```
Check penalty: if val=score + pen*(S-1)^2, derivative penalty 2pen(S-1) (scalar uniform). yes.

Potential issue: if dmax=0 (all zeros), log se? If d all zero, dmax=0, e all 1, val 2/log? se=M, log/beta; gradient B with uniform w: B_i=sum w_k a_{k-i}; if x nonzero, okay. If x all zero, return inf.

`_run_lbfgs`:
```
def _run_lbfgs(a0,beta,pen,time_limit,maxfun_cap):
   a=_norm(a0)
   n=a.size
   t_start=time.monotonic()
   if time_limit<3 or n==0: return _score(a),a
   # calibrate obj
   val,grad=_obj_lse(a,beta,pen)
   dt=time.monotonic()-t_start
   if dt<1e-4: dt=1e-4
   # We want stop before t_start+time_limit*0.85. Use factor 0.75.
   est_maxfun=int(0.75*time_limit/dt)
   est_maxfun=max(20,min(est_maxfun,maxfun_cap,5000))
   maxiter=max(10,est_maxfun//3)
   start=time.monotonic()
   # if time remaining small
   # Use bounds
   try:
      res=minimize(_obj_lse, a, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, args=(beta,pen), bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n, options={'maxfun':est_maxfun,'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8,'maxls':30})
   except Exception: res=None
   if res is not None and getattr(res,'x',None) is not None:
      out=_norm(res.x)
   else: out=a
   # If time exceeded, still return best? L-BFGS may have run longer; check?
   return _score(out), out
```

Also maybe if dt includes first eval and interior uses smaller? okay. To be safer, use `time_limit*0.6`. But then less iteration. We can split into multiple calls: `_run_lbfgs` not check inside. `_subgrad_polish`:
Need avoid using `_obj_lse`? We'll implement. Use `_score_full`.
```
def _subgrad_polish(a0, max_iters=30, deadline=None, active_tol=None):
   a=_norm(a0); best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=_score(a)
   n=a.size
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if deadline and time.monotonic() > deadline: break
      sc,c,d=_score_full(a)
      if not finite: break
      best currently? recompute maybe a not best? Keep.
      dmax=float(d.max())
      if dmax <=0: break
      if active_tol is None: active_tol=max(1e-6, 1e-5*max(1.0,dmax))
      active = d >= dmax - active_tol
      # limit active to at most 256 top if many
      if active.sum() > 256:
         idx=np.argsort(d)[-256:]; active=False; active[idx]=True
      m=int(active.sum())
      w=np.zeros_like(d); w[active]=1.0/m
      B=_cross_corr(a,w)
      S=float(a.sum()); # should 1 but use
      LDS=float(w@d)
      grad=4.0*n*B/(S*S) - 4.0*LDS/S
      grad=grad - np.mean(grad)
      gnorm=float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
      if gnorm<1e-14: break
      # Try scales based on previous? Use list.
      scales=(1e-5,3e-5,1e-4,3e-4,1e-3,3e-3,1e-2,3e-2,0.1,0.3,1.0)
      best_step=None; best_new=best_sc
      for scale in scales:
         if deadline and time.monotonic()>deadline: break
         eta=scale/gnorm
         q=a - eta*grad
         q=np.clip(q,0,None)
         q=_norm(q)
         scq=_score(q)
         if scq<best_new:
            best_new=scq; best_step=q.copy()
      if best_step is None: break
      if best_new<best_sc:
         best_sc=best_new; best_a=best_step.copy(); a=best_step
      else:
         # maybe break if no improvement
         break
   return best_sc,best_a
```
This may be too cautious: If LSE best not exact, first subgrad maybe improves. If no improvement first, break. Could allow a few no-improvements with smaller tol. But okay.

Could use `active_tol` smaller for final: 0.0? Use exact active max: active where d == dmax (within 1e-12). But due numerical, few. Maybe uniform active too narrow misses near peaks. Use 1e-5*dmax? dmax~0.75, tol 7.5e-6. Good.

`quick_polish` pipeline:
```
def _quick_polish(a0, deadline, label, rng):
    a=_norm(a0); best_sc=_score(a); best_a=a
    t0=time.monotonic()
    # first subgrad few
    sc,a_sg=_subgrad_polish(a, max_iters=8, deadline=min(deadline, t0+20)); update
    # L-BFGS stages
    stages=[(20,0.06),(50,0.08),(100,0.10),(300,0.12),(1000,0.16),(3000,0.18),(10000,0.15),(30000,0.15)]
    total_time=max(10.0, deadline-t0-10.0)
    used=0
    for beta,frac in stages:
       stage_end=t0+(used+frac)*total_time; stage_end=min(stage_end,deadline-5)
       budget=stage_end-time.monotonic(); used+=frac
       if budget<10: continue
       n_calls=max(1,min(3,int(budget/25)))
       for ci in range(n_calls):
          if time.monotonic()>stage_end-3: break
          sc,a_new=_run_lbfgs(a,beta,1e-8,budget/n_calls, maxfun_cap=700)
          if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=a_new
          a=_norm(a_new)
          # maybe early if time
    # subgrad final
    if time.monotonic()<deadline-10:
       sc,a_sg=_subgrad_polish(best_a, max_iters=20, deadline=deadline-5); update
    return best_sc,best_a
```
Need avoid using all time if candidate later; deadline alloted. Good.

But `_quick_polish` for each seed will start from that seed; if seed poor, may waste. We can pre-score seeds and choose.

Need implement `_smooth_perturb` robust:
```
def _smooth_perturb(a, rng, strength=0.3):
   n=a.size
   if n<10: return _norm(a)
   # low-pass noise via FFT easier: generate random power with Gaussian falloff and random phase, irfft, crop
   Nf=_next_pow2(n)
   freqs=np.fft.rfftfreq(Nf)*Nf
   width= max(1.0, n / rng.uniform(20,200))? Want smooth medium.
   power=1/(1+(freqs/width)**2)
   power[0]=0
   phase=rng.uniform(-np.pi,np.pi,len(power))
   noise=np.fft.irfft(power*np.exp(1j*phase), Nf)[:n]
   noise = noise - noise.mean()
   q=a + strength*noise
   return _norm(q)
```
This is efficient and smooth. Previous used Gaussian kernel. Use FFT. Need ensure noise std; power not normalized. `strength` relative to a (mean 1/n); if noise std ~? Let's check: power at zero freq set 0; high freq suppressed. Magnitude maybe O(1) if width ~n/50. Then strength * noise may be order 1, too large relative a ~1/n. Need normalize noise to unit variance: `noise /= (np.std(noise)*n?)` We want perturbation RMS relative to mean: after summation, q sum. Let mean a ~1/n. Want perturbation amplitude strength*mean. If noise std ~1, strength*noise type O(1) >> a; clip many zeros. Better set `noise = noise / (np.std(noise)+1e-12) * (1.0/n)`? Then perturbation std strength/n. Use `strength` as fraction of mean. Let's do:
```
noise = np.fft.irfft(...); noise -= mean; sd=noise.std(); if sd>0: noise = noise/sd * (1.0/n)
q=a + strength*noise
```
Then RMS perturbation = strength/n; relative to a mean 1/n is strength. Good.

For power transform, if a has zeros and gamma<1, zeros remain; maybe add eps. Use `a=np.clip(a,1e-12,1e6)**gamma`.

def _make_seed_list(n,parent,rng):
   seeds=[]
   if parent is not None:
      p=_upsample(parent,n)
      seeds.append(('parent',p))
      seeds.append(('parent_s1',_smooth_perturb(p,rng,0.05)))
      seeds.append(('parent_s2',_smooth_perturb(p,rng,0.15)))
      seeds.append(('parent_s3',_smooth_perturb(p,rng,0.35)))
      for g in (0.5,0.7,1.4,2.0):
         a=_power_transform(p,g); seeds.append((f'power{g}',a))
   # Fourier detects
   for k in range(3): seeds.append((f'spec{k}',_spectral_init(n,rng)))
   # deterministic sine
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
   for f in (1,2,3):
     for amp in (0.5,1.0):
       a=1.0+amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*x)
       a=np.clip(a,1e-8,None); a=a/a.sum(); seeds.append((f'sine{f}_{amp}',a))
   # exponent noise

      noise=_smooth_perturb? Actually base uniform plus noise: `sig=exp(smooth_noise)`.

   # uniform
   seeds.append(('uniform',np.ones(n)/n))
   # score
   out=[]
   for name,a in seeds:
      a=_norm(a)
      sc=_score(a)
      if np.isfinite(sc): out.append((sc,name,a))
   out.sort()
   return out
```

`_make_fft_noise(n,rng)` top-level:
```
def _make_fft_noise(n,rng,width_factor=None):
   Nf=_next_pow2(n)
   freqs=np.fft.rfftfreq(Nf)*Nf
   if width_factor is None: width_factor=rng.uniform(10,100)
   width=max(1.0, n/width_factor)
   power=1.0/(1.0+(freqs/width)**2)
   power[0]=0.0
   phase=rng.uniform(-np.pi,np.pi,len(power))
   sig=np.fft.irfft(power*np.exp(1j*phase), Nf)[:n]
   sig -= sig.mean()
   sd=float(sig.std())
   if sd>0: sig=sig/sd
   # Return with amplitude 1/n? perhaps caller uses.
   return sig * (1.0/n)
```
`_smooth_perturb` uses this.

def _spectral_init(n,rng,exponent=None):

   power=1/(1+abs(freqs))**exponent

def _select_diverse(scored, max_cands):
   chosen=[]; ch_arr=[]
   for sc,name,a in scored:
      if len>=max: break
      diverse=True
      for b in ch_arr:
         # normalized correlation
         a0=a-a.mean(); b0=b-b.mean(); denom=np.linalg.norm(a0)*np.linalg.norm(b0)
         if denom>0 and np.dot(a0,b0)/denom > 0.995: diverse=False; break
      if diverse: chosen.append((sc,name,a)); ch_arr.append(a)
   if len<max: fill from scored
   return chosen
```

```
def _try_step(a, grad, best_sc, deadline):
    gnorm=float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
    if gnorm<1e-14: return best_sc,a
    scales=(1e-5,3e-5,1e-4,3e-4,1e-3,3e-3,1e-2,3e-2,0.1,0.3,1.0)
    best_new=best_sc; best_step=a
    for scale in scales:
       if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       eta=scale/gnorm
       q=a - eta*grad
       q=np.maximum(q,0.0)
       q=_norm(q)
       sc=_score(q)
       if sc < best_new:
           best_new=sc; best_step=q.copy()
    return best_new, best_step
```

```
def _cross_corr(a,w):
   n=a.size; if n==0 return
   # only valid if w.size >= 2n-1? if w smaller pad? We'll assume. If w shorter, maybe use full. Safer:
   M_out=2*n-1
   N=_next_pow2(M_out)
   # pad w to N; if w.size>N, truncate? shouldn't.
   if w.size < N:
       wN=np.zeros(N); wN[:w.size]=w
   elif w.size > N:
       # fallback to linear size? use M=n+w.size-1; N=next_pow2(M) and full linear? But not needed. For safety:
       N2=_next_pow2(n+w.size-1); ...
   else wN=w
   A=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N) # rfft pads to N
   W=np.fft.rfft(wN, N)
   out=np.fft.irfft(A*W,N)
   return out[n-1:2*n-1]
```
But if w.size=2n-1 and N next_pow2(2n-1) may be larger than w.size (if not power). `np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],N)` pads. W pad zeros. Good.

```
def _cross_direct(a,w):
   # w length 2n-1, find idx w !=0
   idx=np.nonzero(w)[0]
   if idx.size > max_direct: use FFT
   out=np.zeros(n)
   for k in idx:
       wk=w[k]
       # add wk * a[k-i] for i=0..n-1 valid k-i in [0,n-1]
       i_min=max(0, k-(n-1))
       i_max=min(n-1,k)
       # a slice [i_min:i_max+1], index in w = k - i
       out[i_min:i_max+1] += wk * a[i_min:i_max+1]
   return out
```
This loop in Python over active k; if k<=64, okay. For low β dense, FFT. In `_cross_corr`, we can check nonzero count. But counting nonzero in w of length 14000 cheap.   n=a.size
  if n==0: return empty
  if w.size==0: return zeros

  if w.size >= 2*n - 1:
      # We only need outputs n-1..2n-2. If w has support length >=2n-1, but if extra length nonzero, wrap? If extra beyond 2n-2 could affect outputs? For t in range, y index t-i ≤2n-2; extra >2n-2 not used, but cyclic wrap from y negative? none. So can truncate w to 2n-1.

Function `_make_fft_noise`: If n small. Use `width_factor = rng.uniform(10,100)` maybe. If `width_factor` too small, high freq; okay.
```
def _make_fft_noise(n, rng):
    if n < 4:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    Nf = _next_pow2(n)
    freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(Nf) * Nf
    width = max(1.0, n / float(rng.uniform(10, 100)))
    power = 1.0 / (1.0 + (freqs / width) ** 2)
    power[0] = 0.0
    phase = rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi, size=len(power))
    sig = np.fft.irfft(power * np.exp(1j*phase), Nf)[:n]
    sig -= sig.mean()
    sd = float(np.linalg.norm(sig))
    if sd > 1e-30:
        sig = sig / sd * (1.0 / float(n))
    else:
        sig = np.zeros(n)
    return sig
```
If sig std zero, noise zero.

`_smooth_perturb`: noise as above; q=a+strength*noise; clip norm. If noise zero, return a.

`_spectral_init`:
```
def _spectral_init(n,rng,exponent=None):
   if n<2: return np.array([1.0])
   if exponent is None: exponent=rng.uniform(0.4,1.8)
   Nf=_next_pow2(n)
   freqs=np.fft.rfftfreq(Nf)*Nf
   power=1.0/(1.0+np.abs(freqs))**exponent
   power[0]=0.0
   phase=rng.uniform(-np.pi,np.pi,len(power))
   sig=np.fft.irfft(power*np.exp(1j*phase), Nf)[:n]
   sig=sig-sig.min()
   if sig.max() <=0: return np.ones(n)/n
   return _norm(sig)
```
Could be all negative? shift.

`_power_transform`:
```
def _power_transform(a,gamma):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   a=np.nan_to_num(...)
   a=np.clip(a,1e-12,None)
   a=np.power(a,gamma)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<1e-12: return np.full(a.size,1/a.size)
   return a/s
```

Implement `_run_lbfgs` with global set:
```
def _run_lbfgs(a0,beta,pen,time_limit,maxfun_cap):
    global _CUR_BETA,_CUR_PEN
    a=_norm(a0)
    n=a.size
    if n==0 or time_limit<3: return _score(a),a
    _CUR_BETA=float(max(1.0,beta))
    _CUR_PEN=float(pen)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    try: val,grad=_obj_lse(a)
    except Exception: return _score(a),a
    dt=time.monotonic()-t0
    if dt<1e-4: dt=1e-4
    mf=int(0.55*time_limit/dt)
    mf=max(20,min(mf,int(maxfun_cap),5000))
    maxiter=max(10,mf//3)
    start_t=time.monotonic()
    try:
       res=minimize(_obj_lse,a,method='L-BFGS-B',jac=True,bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n,options={'maxfun':mf,'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8,'maxls':40})
    except Exception:
       res=None
    out=a
    if res is not None and getattr(res,'x',None) is not None:
       out=_norm(res.x)
    # If L-BFGS stopped because time exceeded, maybe res.x? okay.
    sc=_score(out)
    if not np.isfinite(sc): sc=_score(a); out=a.copy()
    return sc,out
```
Global variables set; after minimize, if next call sets different. Good.

`_lse_score_grad_raw`:
Need pass beta>0. If beta<1 set 1.
```
def _lse_score_grad_raw(a,beta,pen):
   n=a.size
   if n==0: return 1e10, np.array([])
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   a=np.nan_to_num(a,nan=0.0,posinf=1e6,neginf=0.0)
   a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
   S=float(a.sum())
   if S<1e-8: return 1e10, np.zeros(n)
   c=_self_conv(a); d=float(n)*c/(S*S); d=np.maximum(d,0.0)
   dmax=float(d.max())
   if not np.isfinite(dmax): return 1e10, zeros
   z=beta*(d-dmax)
   z=np.clip(z,-745.0,0.0)
   e=np.exp(z)
   e[z < -60.0] = 0.0
   se=float(e.sum())
   if se <=0 or not np.isfinite(se):
      w=np.zeros_like(d); im=int(np.argmax(d)); w[im]=1.0; logse=0.0
   else:
      w=e/se; logse=float(np.log(se))
   val=2.0*(dmax + logse/float(beta))
   B=_cross_corr(a,w,32)
   LDS=float(np.dot(w,d))
   grad=4.0*float(n)*B/(S*S) - 4.0*LDS/S
   if pen>0:
      val += pen*(S-1.0)*(S-1.0)
      grad = grad + 2.0*pen*(S-1.0)
   grad=np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
   return float(val), grad
```
`e[z < -60] = 0` may modify e; if all? se. Good.

`_obj_lse(x)` simply `return _lse_score_grad_raw(x,_CUR_BETA,_CUR_PEN)`.

`_cross_corr` with max_direct:
```
def _cross_corr(a,w,max_direct=32):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel(); w=np.asarray(w,float).ravel()
   n=a.size
   if n==0: return np.empty(0,float)
   if w.size==0: return np.zeros(n,float)
   if a.size != n? no.
   # Choose limited length for w: only first 2n-1 matter
   M=2*n-1
   if max_direct >=0:
      # For direct, consider only indices 0..min(M,w.size)-1 and nonzero.
      limit=min(M,w.size)
      idx=np.nonzero(w[:limit])[0]
      if 0 < idx.size <= max_direct:
         out=np.zeros(n,float)
         for k in idx:
             wk=float(w[k])
             if wk==0.0: continue
             i_min = 0
             tmp = k - (n-1)
             if tmp > i_min: i_min=tmp
             i_max = n-1
             if k < i_max: i_max=k
             if i_min <= i_max:
                 j_start = k - i_max
                 j_stop = k - i_min + 1
                 out[i_min:i_max+1] += wk * a[j_start:j_stop][::-1]
         return np.nan_to_num(out,...)
   N=_next_pow2(M)
   wN=np.zeros(N,float)
   lead=min(w.size,N)
   if lead>0: wN[:lead]=w[:lead]
   Ar=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],N)
   W=np.fft.rfft(wN,N)
   out=np.fft.irfft(Ar*W,N)
   res=out[n-1:2*n-1]
   return np.nan_to_num(res,...)
```
Check direct for k > M? If w size >M, idx limit first M. Good.
If `max_direct=0`, skip direct. Default 32. In `_lse_score_grad_raw` passing 32. In subgrad maybe pass 1 for single? We can call `_cross_corr(a,w,1000)`? But if active uniform 100, direct 100*7000=700k Python, maybe okay; FFT maybe faster. Don't pass huge. For single w, idx 1 direct.

But in direct, slicing `a[j_start:j_stop][::-1]` creates copy. For 32 peaks length 7000, 224k copy, okay. For 100, 700k. Good.

def _score_full(a):
   a=np.asarray... clip

   if S<... return inf, empty? Need return c,d for subgrad. If fail, return inf, None, None.
   c=_self_conv(a)

`_try_step`:
```
def _try_step(a, grad, best_sc, deadline):
    grad=np.asarray(grad,float).ravel()
    gnorm=float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
    if gnorm<1e-14 or not np.isfinite(gnorm): return best_sc,a
    scales=(1e-6,3e-6,1e-5,3e-5,1e-4,3e-4,1e-3,3e-3,0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1.0)
    best_new=best_sc; best_step=a
    for scale in scales:
       if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       eta=scale/gnorm
       q=a - eta*grad
       q=np.maximum(q,0.0)
       q=_norm(q)
       sc=_score(q)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_new:
           best_new=sc; best_step=q.copy()
    return best_new,best_step
```
If q all zero, norm uniform maybe changes; score maybe high. Fine.

`_subgrad_polish(a0,max_iters,deadline,active_tol)`:
```
def _subgrad_polish(a0,max_iters=30,deadline=None,active_tol=None):
   a=_norm(a0); best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=_score(a); n=a.size
   if active_tol is None: active_tol=1e-5
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline: break
      sc,c,d=_score_full(a)
      if not np.isfinite(sc) or d is None: break
      if sc < best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy()
      if d is None: break
      dmax=float(d.max())
      if dmax <= 1e-15: break
      tol=max(1e-8, active_tol*max(1.0,dmax))
      active = d >= dmax - tol
      if not active.any(): active[int(np.argmax(d))]=True
      if int(active.sum()) > 128:
         idx=np.argsort(d)[-128:]
         active=np.zeros_like(d,bool); active[idx]=True
      w=np.zeros_like(d); w[active] = 1.0/float(active.sum())
      B=_cross_corr(a,w,32)
      S=float(a.sum())
      LDS=float(np.dot(w,d))
      grad = 4.0*float(n)*B/(S*S) - 4.0*LDS/S
      grad = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
      new_sc,new_a=_try_step(a,grad,best_sc,deadline)
      if new_sc < best_sc:
          a=new_a.copy()
      else:
          # try single top peak
          imax=int(np.argmax(d))
          w1=np.zeros_like(d); w1[imax]=1.0
          B1=_cross_corr(a,w1,1)
          LDS1=float(d[imax])
          grad1 = 4.0*n*B1/(S*S) - 4.0*LDS1/S
          grad1 = grad1 - np.mean(grad1)
          new_sc1,new_a1=_try_step(a,grad1,best_sc,deadline)
          if new_sc1 < best_sc:
              a=new_a1.copy()
          else:
              break
      best_sc,_ = _score_full(a)? If _try_step updated best_sc maybe not a? Wait _try_step returns best_new and step with best_new. If new_sc<best_sc, set a. But best_sc may not updated to new_sc? Need update:
          best_sc new_sc; best_a new_a.
```

def _quick_polish(a0, deadline, label, rng):
   global? no
   a=_norm(a0)
   best_sc=_score(a); best_a=a.copy(); n=a.size
   print(f"[{label}] start n={n} score={best_sc:.8f}")
   t0=time.monotonic()
   # initial subgrad small maybe 5
   if time.monotonic() < deadline - 20:
      sc_sg,a_sg=_subgrad_polish(a, max_iters=6, deadline=min(deadline, t0+20), active_tol=1e-4)
      if sc_sg<best_sc: update
      a=_norm(a_sg) if sc_sg<best_sc+0.02 else best_a
   # stages
   stages=[(50,0.05),(100,0.07),(300,0.10),(1000,0.14),(3000,0.18),(10000,0.20),(30000,0.16),(100000,0.10)]
   total_stage = max(10.0, deadline - t0 - 20.0) * 0.78
   used=0.0
   for beta,frac in stages:
      if time.monotonic() > deadline - 15: break
      stage_time=frac*total_stage
      stage_end=min(deadline -10, time.monotonic()+stage_time)  # maybe not sequential? We can just budget.
      if time.monotonic() >= stage_end -1: continue
      n_calls=max(1,min(3,int(stage_time/25)+1))
      budget=stage_time/n_calls
      for ci in range(n_calls):
         if time.monotonic() > stage_end -2: break
         sc,a_new=_run_lbfgs(a,beta,1e-8,budget,1500)
         a_new=_norm(a_new)
         if sc < best_sc:
             best_sc=sc; best_a=a_new.copy(); print
         if sc < best_sc + 0.08:
             a=a_new
         else:
             a=best_a.copy()
      # maybe after each stage, subgrad a bit?
      if time.monotonic() < deadline - 30:
         sc_sg,a_sg=_subgrad_polish(a,max_iters=5,deadline=min(deadline-5,time.monotonic()+20),active_tol=1e-5)
         if sc_sg<best_sc: update; a=a_sg
   # final subgrad with remaining
   if time.monotonic() < deadline - 10:
      sc_sg,a_sg=_subgrad_polish(best_a, max_iters=30, deadline=deadline-5, active_tol=1e-5)
      if sc_sg<best_sc: update
   print end
   return best_sc,best_a

Direct formula earlier robust: c_raw derivative =2 B_raw (B_raw=∑w a_raw_{k-i}=S B_z). d_raw=n c_raw/S^2. ∂d_raw/∂a_i = n(2B_raw)/S^2 -2n c_raw S /S^4 =2n S B_z/S^2 -2n S^2 c_z /S^3 =2n B_z/S -2n c_z/S =2/S (n B_z - n c_z). ∑w =2/S (n B_z - n c_w) where c_w =∑w c_z. Since d_w = n c_w = LDS. So ∂d_raw = (2/S)(n B_z - LDS). Score=2LSE => grad =2 * ∑w ∂d = (4/S)(n B_z - LDS) = 4n B_z/S -4 LDS/S. Yes. Good. No center term. This is exact. It also should satisfy a·grad=0: a·G/S = S z·(4nB_z -4LDS)/S =4n z·B_z -4LDS. Is z·B_z =? ∑z_i B_z_i =∑_i∑_k w_k z_i z_{k-i} =∑_k w_k ∑_i z_i z_{k-i} =∑w_k c_{z,k}=LDS/n. Thus z·B_z=LDS/n, so dot=0. Good.
Thus use raw gradient exact. No center necessary. But for subgrad on simplex we project separately.

So `_lse_score_grad_raw` normalized computation:

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative sequence minimizing
    2 * len(a) * max(convolve(a, a)) / (sum(a)^2).

Approach:
- Start from any previous construction if available, plus diverse parametric,
  spectral, and perturbed seeds.
- Optimize a smooth log-sum-exp surrogate for the max-convolution objective.
- Use a speed-up: the gradient cross-correlation only needs outputs
  n-1..2n-2 and can be computed with an FFT of size 2n-1 instead of 3n-2.
- Add exact active-set subgradient polishing on the true max objective.
- Include a log-domain L-BFGS polish to avoid zero-boundary stagnation.
- Length scaling / restarting is used to escape local minima and improve
  the converged value.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

# Globals used by the SciPy objective callbacks to avoid closures.
_CUR_BETA = 1.0
_CUR_PEN = 0.0
_CUR_PEN_Y = 0.0


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _norm(a, target=1.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return np.full(a.size, target / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a * (float(target) / s)


def _self_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    N = _next_pow2(m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:m]
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return np.maximum(out, 0.0)


def _cross_corr(a, w, max_direct=32):
    """
    Returns B_i = sum_k w_k a_{k-i} for i=0..n-1, with valid indices only.
    This is the derivative building block for c_k = sum_i a_i a_{k-i}.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if w.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    m = 2 * n - 1

    # Direct method for very sparse weights.
    if max_direct and max_direct >= 1:
        limit = min(m, w.size)
        idx = np.nonzero(w[:limit])[0]
        if 0 < idx.size <= max_direct:
            out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            for kk in idx:
                k = int(kk)
                wk = float(w[k])
                if wk == 0.0:
                    continue
                i_min = max(0, k - (n - 1))
                i_max = min(n - 1, k)
                if i_min <= i_max:
                    j_start = k - i_max
                    j_stop = k - i_min + 1
                    out[i_min:i_max + 1] += wk * a[j_start:j_stop][::-1]
            return np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

    # FFT method.  For i in [0,n-1], all needed w-indexes are in [0,2n-2],
    # so an FFT of size a power of two >= 2n-1 is sufficient.
    N = _next_pow2(m)
    wN = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
    lead = min(w.size, N)
    if lead > 0:
        wN[:lead] = w[:lead]
    A = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    W = np.fft.rfft(wN, N)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * W, N)
    res = out[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    return np.nan_to_num(res, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)


def _score_full(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or S <= 1e-12:
        return np.inf, np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64), np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    z = a / S
    c = _self_conv(z)
    d = float(n) * c
    d = np.maximum(d, 0.0)
    dm = float(d.max()) if d.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(dm):
        return np.inf, c, d
    return float(2.0 * dm), c, d


def _score(a):
    return _score_full(a)[0]


def _upsample(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    m = a.size
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if m == n:
        return a.copy()
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, dtype=np.float64)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.interp(x1, x0, a).astype(np.float64)


def _make_fft_noise(n, rng):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if n < 4:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    Nf = _next_pow2(n)
    freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(Nf) * Nf
    width = max(1.0, n / float(rng.uniform(10.0, 100.0)))
    power = 1.0 / (1.0 + (freqs / width) ** 2)
    power[0] = 0.0
    phase = rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi, size=len(power))
    sig = np.fft.irfft(power * np.exp(1j * phase), Nf)[:n]
    sig -= sig.mean()
    sd = float(np.linalg.norm(sig))
    if sd > 1e-30:
        sig = sig / sd * (1.0 / float(n))
    else:
        sig = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return sig


def _smooth_perturb(a, rng, strength=0.25):
    a = _norm(a)
    if a.size < 4:
        return a
    noise = _make_fft_noise(a.size, rng)
    q = a + float(strength) * noise
    return _norm(q)


def _power_transform(a, gamma):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    try:
        b = np.power(a, float(gamma))
    except Exception:
        return _norm(a)
    b = np.nan_to_num(b, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return b / s


def _spectral_init(n, rng, exponent=None):
    n = int(max(2, n))
    if exponent is None:
        exponent = float(rng.uniform(0.35, 1.70))
    Nf = _next_pow2(n)
    freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(Nf) * Nf
    power = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.abs(freqs)) ** exponent
    power[0] = 0.0
    phase = rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi, size=len(power))
    sig = np.fft.irfft(power * np.exp(1j * phase), Nf)[:n]
    sig = sig - sig.min()
    if sig.max() <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return _norm(sig)


def _make_seed_list(n, parent, rng):
    n = int(max(8, n))
    seeds = []

    if parent is not None:
        p = _upsample(parent, n)
        p = _norm(p)
        seeds.append(("parent", p))
        seeds.append(("parent_s1", _smooth_perturb(p, rng, 0.04)))
        seeds.append(("parent_s2", _smooth_perturb(p, rng, 0.12)))
        seeds.append(("parent_s3", _smooth_perturb(p, rng, 0.30)))
        for g in (0.55, 0.75, 1.35, 1.80):
            seeds.append(("power_" + str(g), _power_transform(p, g)))

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False, dtype=np.float64)

    # Low-frequency modulations.
    for f in (1, 2, 3):
        for amp in (0.5, 0.9, 1.3):
            for ph in (0.0, np.pi / 2.0):
                a = 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + ph)
                a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
                if float(a.sum()) > 0.0:
                    seeds.append(("cos_" + str(f) + "_" + str(amp) + "_" + str(ph), a / a.sum()))

    # Edge-weighted parametric seeds.
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for g in (-0.5, 0.5, 1.0):
        a = np.power(idx + 1.0, g) * np.power(n - idx, g)
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        if float(a.sum()) > 0.0:
            seeds.append(("edge_" + str(g), a / a.sum()))
    for g in (-1.0, 1.0):
        a = np.exp(g * (idx / float(max(1, n - 1)) - 0.5))
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        if float(a.sum()) > 0.0:
            seeds.append(("radial_" + str(g), a / a.sum()))

    # Random spectral seeds.
    for k in range(4):
        seeds.append(("spec_" + str(k), _spectral_init(n, rng)))

    # Smoothed random positive seeds.
    for k in range(2):
        noise = _make_fft_noise(n, rng)
        a = np.exp(3.0 * noise)
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        if float(a.sum()) > 0.0:
            seeds.append(("expnoise_" + str(k), a / a.sum()))

    # Uniform fallback.
    seeds.append(("uniform", np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)))

    scored = []
    for name, a in seeds:
        a = _norm(a)
        sc = _score(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((float(sc), name, a))

    scored.sort()
    return scored


def _lse_score_grad_raw(a, beta, pen):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return 1e10, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-8:
        return 1e10, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    # Work with the normalized sequence to avoid overflow; the final gradient
    # is mapped back to the raw coordinates.
    z = a / S
    c = _self_conv(z)
    d = float(n) * c
    d = np.maximum(d, 0.0)
    dmax = float(d.max()) if d.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(dmax):
        return 1e10, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    beta = max(1.0, float(beta))
    zz = beta * (d - dmax)
    zz = np.clip(zz, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(zz)
    e[zz < -60.0] = 0.0
    se = float(e.sum())

    if se <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(se):
        w = np.zeros_like(d)
        im = int(np.argmax(d))
        w[im] = 1.0
        logse = 0.0
    else:
        w = e / se
        logse = float(np.log(se))

    val = 2.0 * (dmax + logse / beta)

    B = _cross_corr(z, w, 32)
    LDS = float(np.dot(w, d))

    # Gradient of smooth score for raw a:
    # grad = (4 n B_z - 4 LDS) / S
    grad = (4.0 * float(n) * B - 4.0 * LDS) / S

    if pen > 0.0:
        eS = S - 1.0
        val += float(pen) * eS * eS
        grad = grad + (2.0 * float(pen) * eS)

    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
    return float(val), grad


def _obj_lse(x):
    return _lse_score_grad_raw(x, _CUR_BETA, _CUR_PEN)


def _run_lbfgs(a0, beta, pen, time_limit, maxfun_cap):
    global _CUR_BETA, _CUR_PEN

    a = _norm(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or time_limit < 3.0:
        return _score(a), a

    _CUR_BETA = float(max(1.0, beta))
    _CUR_PEN = float(max(0.0, pen))

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        v, g = _obj_lse(a)
    except Exception:
        return _score(a), a
    dt = time.monotonic() - t0
    if dt < 1e-4:
        dt = 1e-4

    mf = int(0.55 * max(1.0, time_limit) / dt)
    mf = max(20, min(mf, int(maxfun_cap), 5000))
    maxiter = max(10, mf // 3)

    out = a
    try:
        res = minimize(
            _obj_lse,
            a,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=[(0.0, None)] * n,
            options={
                "maxfun": mf,
                "maxiter": maxiter,
                "ftol": 1e-12,
                "gtol": 1e-8,
                "maxls": 40,
            },
        )
        if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
            cand = _norm(res.x)
            cand_sc = _score(cand)
            if np.isfinite(cand_sc):
                out = cand
    except Exception:
        out = a

    sc = _score(out)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        out = a.copy()
        sc = _score(out)
    return float(sc), out


def _obj_lse_y(y):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = y.size
    if n == 0:
        return 1e10, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=30.0, neginf=-30.0)
    y = np.clip(y, -30.0, 30.0)
    yy = y - float(np.mean(y))

    a = np.exp(yy)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-8:
        return 1e10, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / s

    val, ga = _lse_score_grad_raw(a, _CUR_BETA, 0.0)
    grad_y = a * ga

    if _CUR_PEN_Y > 0.0:
        val += float(_CUR_PEN_Y) * float(np.dot(yy, yy))
        grad_y = grad_y + (2.0 * float(_CUR_PEN_Y)) * yy

    grad_y = np.nan_to_num(grad_y, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
    return float(val), grad_y


def _run_lbfgs_log(a0, beta, pen_y, time_limit, maxfun_cap):
    global _CUR_BETA, _CUR_PEN_Y

    a = _norm(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or time_limit < 3.0:
        return _score(a), a

    y = np.log(np.clip(a, 1e-12, 1e12))
    y = y - float(np.mean(y))
    y = np.clip(y, -30.0, 30.0)

    _CUR_BETA = float(max(1.0, beta))
    _CUR_PEN_Y = float(max(0.0, pen_y))

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        v, g = _obj_lse_y(y)
    except Exception:
        return _score(a), a
    dt = time.monotonic() - t0
    if dt < 1e-4:
        dt = 1e-4

    mf = int(0.55 * max(1.0, time_limit) / dt)
    mf = max(20, min(mf, int(maxfun_cap), 5000))
    maxiter = max(10, mf // 3)

    out_a = a
    try:
        res = minimize(
            _obj_lse_y,
            y,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=[(-30.0, 30.0)] * n,
            options={
                "maxfun": mf,
                "maxiter": maxiter,
                "ftol": 1e-12,
                "gtol": 1e-8,
                "maxls": 40,
            },
        )
        if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
            yy = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            yy = yy - float(np.mean(yy))
            yy = np.clip(yy, -30.0, 30.0)
            cand = _norm(np.exp(yy))
            cand_sc = _score(cand)
            if np.isfinite(cand_sc):
                out_a = cand
    except Exception:
        out_a = a

    sc = _score(out_a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        out_a = a.copy()
        sc = _score(out_a)
    return float(sc), out_a


def _try_step(a, grad, best_sc, deadline):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    grad = np.asarray(grad, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
    if gnorm <= 1e-14 or not np.isfinite(gnorm):
        return float(best_sc), a

    scales = (1e-6, 3e-6, 1e-5, 3e-5, 1e-4, 3e-4, 1e-3, 3e-3, 0.01, 0.03, 0.1, 0.3, 1.0)
    best_new = float(best_sc)
    best_x = a

    for scale in scales:
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        eta = float(scale) / gnorm
        q = a - eta * grad
        q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
        q = _norm(q)
        sc = _score(q)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_new:
            best_new = float(sc)
            best_x = q.copy()

    return best_new, best_x


def _subgrad_polish(a0, max_iters=30, deadline=None, active_tol=1e-5):
    a = _norm(a0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return best_sc, best_a

    for _ in range(int(max(1, max_iters))):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        sc, c, d = _score_full(a)
        if not np.isfinite(sc) or d is None or d.size == 0:
            break
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = a.copy()

        dmax = float(d.max())
        if dmax <= 1e-15:
            break

        tol = max(1e-10, float(active_tol) * max(1.0, dmax))
        active = d >= (dmax - tol)
        if not active.any():
            active[int(np.argmax(d))] = True

        am = int(active.sum())
        if am > 128:
            idx = np.argsort(d)[-128:]
            active = np.zeros_like(d, dtype=bool)
            active[idx] = True
            am = int(active.sum())

        w = np.zeros_like(d)
        w[active] = 1.0 / float(am)

        S = float(a.sum())
        B = _cross_corr(a, w, 32)
        LDS = float(np.dot(w, d))
        grad = 4.0 * float(n) * B / S - 4.0 * LDS / S
        grad = grad - float(np.mean(grad))

        new_sc, new_a = _try_step(a, grad, best_sc, deadline)
        if np.isfinite(new_sc) and new_sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(new_sc)
            best_a = new_a.copy()
            a = best_a.copy()
            continue

        # Try the single tallest peak if uniform active set stalls.
        im = int(np.argmax(d))
        w1 = np.zeros_like(d)
        w1[im] = 1.0
        B1 = _cross_corr(a, w1, 1)
        LDS1 = float(d[im])
        grad1 = 4.0 * float(n) * B1 / S - 4.0 * LDS1 / S
        grad1 = grad1 - float(np.mean(grad1))
        new_sc1, new_a1 = _try_step(a, grad1, best_sc, deadline)

        if np.isfinite(new_sc1) and new_sc1 < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(new_sc1)
            best_a = new_a1.copy()
            a = best_a.copy()
            continue

        break

    return best_sc, best_a


def _quick_polish(a0, deadline, label, rng):
    a = _norm(a0)
    n = a.size
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    print("[{}] start n={} score={:.8f}".format(label, n, best_sc), flush=True)

    # Small amount of exact polishing before smooth optimization.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 20.0:
        sc_sg, a_sg = _subgrad_polish(
            a,
            max_iters=6,
            deadline=min(deadline, t0 + 20.0),
            active_tol=1e-4,
        )
        if sc_sg < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc_sg)
            best_a = a_sg.copy()
            print("[{}] subgrad start: {:.8f}".format(label, best_sc), flush=True)
        if sc_sg < best_sc + 0.03:
            a = _norm(a_sg)
        else:
            a = best_a.copy()

    # Smooth LSE stages.  Each stage receives a fraction of the remaining time.
    stages = [
        (50.0, 0.04),
        (100.0, 0.05),
        (300.0, 0.08),
        (1000.0, 0.12),
        (3000.0, 0.16),
        (10000.0, 0.20),
        (30000.0, 0.22),
        (100000.0, 0.13),
    ]

    maxfun_cap = 1600 if n <= 9000 else 1200

    for beta, frac in stages:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 25.0:
            break
        rem = max(10.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 20.0)
        budget = float(frac) * rem
        if budget < 8.0:
            continue

        n_calls = max(1, min(3, int(budget / 25.0) + 1))
        per_call = budget / float(n_calls)

        for _ci in range(n_calls):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 15.0:
                break
            sc, a_new = _run_lbfgs(a, beta, 1e-8, per_call, maxfun_cap)
            a_new = _norm(a_new)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(sc)
                best_a = a_new.copy()
                print("[{}] beta={} improvement: {:.8f}".format(label, beta, best_sc), flush=True)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc + 0.08:
                a = a_new
            else:
                a = best_a.copy()

        # Light active-set cleanup after each stage.
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
            sc_sg, a_sg = _subgrad_polish(
                a,
                max_iters=4,
                deadline=min(deadline - 10.0, time.monotonic() + 15.0),
                active_tol=1e-5,
            )
            if sc_sg < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(sc_sg)
                best_a = a_sg.copy()
                print("[{}] subgrad beta~{}: {:.8f}".format(label, beta, best_sc), flush=True)
            if sc_sg < best_sc + 0.02:
                a = _norm(a_sg)
            else:
                a = best_a.copy()

    # Final exact polishing in this candidate's allotted time.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 10.0:
        sc_sg, a_sg = _subgrad_polish(
            best_a,
            max_iters=40,
            deadline=deadline - 5.0,
            active_tol=1e-6,
        )
        if sc_sg < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc_sg)
            best_a = a_sg.copy()
            print("[{}] final subgrad: {:.8f}".format(label, best_sc), flush=True)

    print("[{}] end n={} score={:.8f} elapsed={:.1f}s".format(label, n, best_sc, time.monotonic() - t0), flush=True)
    return best_sc, best_a


def _final_polish(a0, deadline, rng):
    a = _norm(a0)
    n = a.size
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)
    print("=== final polish n={} score={:.8f} ===".format(n, best_sc), flush=True)

    maxfun_cap = 1200 if n <= 9000 else 900

    # Higher-beta smooth polishing.
    stages = [
        (5000.0, 0.16),
        (15000.0, 0.16),
        (50000.0, 0.20),
        (150000.0, 0.18),
    ]
    for beta, frac in stages:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 50.0:
            break
        rem = max(20.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 40.0)
        budget = float(frac) * rem
        if budget < 18.0:
            continue
        sc, a_new = _run_lbfgs(a, beta, 1e-8, budget, maxfun_cap)
        a_new = _norm(a_new)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = a_new.copy()
            print("  final LSE beta={} score={:.8f}".format(beta, best_sc), flush=True)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc + 0.02:
            a = a_new
        else:
            a = best_a.copy()

    # Log-domain polish (avoids zero-boundary artifacts).
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 100.0 and n <= 20000:
        rem = max(30.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 90.0)
        budget = 0.25 * rem
        if budget > 25.0:
            sc, a_new = _run_lbfgs_log(best_a, 20000.0, 1e-5, budget, 900)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(sc)
                best_a = a_new.copy()
                print("  final log LSE score={:.8f}".format(best_sc), flush=True)

    # Exact active-set subgradient polishing.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
        sc_sg, a_sg = _subgrad_polish(
            best_a,
            max_iters=80,
            deadline=deadline - 20.0,
            active_tol=1e-7,
        )
        if sc_sg < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc_sg)
            best_a = a_sg.copy()
            print("  final subgrad score={:.8f}".format(best_sc), flush=True)

    # A few local restarts around the current best.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 110.0:
        strengths = (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01)
        for s in strengths:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 90.0:
                break
            q = _smooth_perturb(best_a, rng, s)
            rem = max(30.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 80.0)
            budget = min(80.0, max(25.0, 0.25 * rem))
            sc, q = _run_lbfgs(q, 5000.0, 1e-8, budget, 700)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(sc)
                best_a = q.copy()
                print("  restart strength={} score={:.8f}".format(s, best_sc), flush=True)

    # Last exact polish.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 8.0:
        sc_sg, a_sg = _subgrad_polish(
            best_a,
            max_iters=50,
            deadline=deadline - 4.0,
            active_tol=1e-7,
        )
        if sc_sg < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc_sg)
            best_a = a_sg.copy()
            print("  very final subgrad score={:.8f}".format(best_sc), flush=True)

    return best_sc, best_a


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Main entry point.  Returns a list of non-negative floats minimizing
    evaluate_sequence(sequence).
    """
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    budget = float(budget_s)
    if budget <= 60.0 or not np.isfinite(budget):
        budget = 60.0
    budget = min(budget, 2400.0)
    deadline = t_start + budget - 45.0
    if deadline <= t_start + 90.0:
        deadline = t_start + max(90.0, budget - 5.0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if np.isfinite(seed) else 42)

    # Load previous construction if available.
    parent = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if p.size >= 16 and float(p.sum()) > 0.01:
                parent = _norm(p)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    # Initial best.
    best_a = None
    best_sc = np.inf
    if parent is not None:
        best_a = _norm(parent)
        best_sc = _score(best_a)
    if best_a is None or not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        best_a = np.ones(8192, dtype=np.float64) / 8192.0
        best_sc = _score(best_a)

    print("Parent available: {}, initial score={:.8f}".format(parent is not None, best_sc), flush=True)

    # Main working length.
    if parent is not None:
        pn = parent.size
        if pn < 1024:
            n0 = 8192
        elif pn <= 12000:
            n0 = pn
        else:
            n0 = 12288
    else:
        n0 = 8192
    n0 = int(min(max(1024, n0), 12288))

    # Optional second length for scaling and escape.
    if n0 <= 8192:
        n1 = 8192 if n0 < 8192 else 12288
    elif n0 <= 10000:
        n1 = 12288
    else:
        n1 = None

    total_window = max(120.0, deadline - t_start)
    if n1 is not None:
        main_deadline = t_start + 0.56 * total_window
        second_deadline = t_start + 0.82 * total_window
    else:
        main_deadline = t_start + 0.78 * total_window
        second_deadline = main_deadline

    # Generate initial candidates at the main length.
    scored = _make_seed_list(n0, parent, rng)
    if not scored:
        scored = [(2.0, "uniform", np.ones(n0, dtype=np.float64) / float(n0))]

    top_count = 4
    if (main_deadline - time.monotonic()) < 500.0:
        top_count = 3
    if (main_deadline - time.monotonic()) < 250.0:
        top_count = 2

    cands = scored[:top_count]

    # Ensure at least one exploratory non-parent seed if possible.
    has_expl = False
    for _sc, _nm, _ar in cands:
        if "spec" in _nm or "cos" in _nm or "edge" in _nm or "radial" in _nm or "noise" in _nm:
            has_expl = True
            break
    if not has_expl:
        for item in scored:
            nm = item[1]
            if "spec" in nm or "cos" in nm or "edge" in nm or "radial" in nm or "noise" in nm:
                cands.append(item)
                break

    # Optimize candidates within main window.
    if len(cands) == 1:
        fracs = (1.0,)
    elif len(cands) == 2:
        fracs = (0.65, 0.35)
    elif len(cands) == 3:
        fracs = (0.52, 0.30, 0.18)
    else:
        fracs = (0.46, 0.26, 0.16, 0.12)

    remaining_main = max(60.0, main_deadline - time.monotonic())
    for i, (sc_init, name, arr) in enumerate(cands):
        if time.monotonic() >= main_deadline - 30.0:
            break
        frac = float(fracs[i]) if i < len(fracs) else 0.1
        sub_deadline = min(
            main_deadline - 10.0,
            time.monotonic() + max(25.0, frac * remaining_main),
        )
        remaining_main = max(20.0, sub_deadline - time.monotonic())

        arr = _norm(arr)
        sc0 = _score(arr)
        if sc0 < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc0)
            best_a = arr.copy()
            print("Seed {} at n={} improves to {:.8f}".format(name, n0, best_sc), flush=True)

        sc, seq = _quick_polish(arr, sub_deadline, "main_{}_{}".format(i, name), rng)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = _norm(seq)
            print("*** global best updated in main: {:.8f} ***".format(best_sc), flush=True)

    # Optional second length: use the current best and possibly the parent again.
    if n1 is not None and time.monotonic() < second_deadline - 80.0:
        a_up = _upsample(best_a, n1)
        sc_up = _score(a_up)
        if sc_up < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc_up)
            best_a = a_up.copy()
            print("Upsampling to n={} improves to {:.8f}".format(n1, best_sc), flush=True)

        remaining2 = max(60.0, second_deadline - time.monotonic())
        sub1 = min(second_deadline - 70.0, time.monotonic() + 0.65 * remaining2)
        sc, seq = _quick_polish(a_up, sub1, "len{}".format(n1), rng)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = _norm(seq)
            print("*** global best updated at n={}: {:.8f} ***".format(n1, best_sc), flush=True)

        if parent is not None and time.monotonic() < second_deadline - 120.0:
            a_p = _upsample(parent, n1)
            sc_p = _score(a_p)
            if sc_p < np.inf + 0.2:  # just a large finite check
                sub2 = second_deadline - 20.0
                if sub2 > time.monotonic() + 80.0:
                    sc, seq = _quick_polish(a_p, sub2, "len{}_parent".format(n1), rng)
                    if sc < best_sc:
                        best_sc = float(sc)
                        best_a = _norm(seq)
                        print("*** global best updated parent n={}: {:.8f} ***".format(n1, best_sc), flush=True)

    # Final polish on the best sequence found.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
        best_sc, best_a = _final_polish(best_a, deadline, rng)

    # Prepare output.
    out = _norm(best_a)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(out.sum())
    if S < 0.01:
        out = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)
        S = float(out.sum())
    out = out / S

    result = [float(x) for x in out]

    verified = best_sc
    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(result))
    except Exception:
        verified = best_sc

    print(
        "FINAL len={} internal={:.8f} verified={:.8f} time={:.1f}s".format(
            len(result), best_sc, verified, time.monotonic() - t_start
        ),
        flush=True,
    )
    return result
```
